         "ascender": 0.800000011920929,
         "descender": -0.20000000298023224,
         "text": "The single-line diagram may be used in a number of important ways in operating and main-",

         "ascender": 0.800000011920929,

         "color": 0,
         "alpha": 255,
         "ascender": 0.800000011920929,
         "descender": -0.20000000298023224,
         "text": "A switching simulator can be very useful for verifying the effects of any set of switching",
         "origin": [
          120.0,
          262.6669921875

          255.8115234375,

         "color": 0,
         "alpha": 255,
         "ascender": 0.800000011920929,
         "descender": -0.20000000298023224,
         "text": "operations. Low-voltage toggle switches are substituted for circuit breakers and disconnect",
         "origin": [

          267.8115234375,
          492.08905029296875,

         "color": 0,
         "alpha": 255,
         "ascender": 0.800000011920929,
         "descender": -0.20000000298023224,
         "text": "switches. Signal lamps indicate whether a circuit path has been omitted in the switching",
         "origin": [
          120.0,
          286.6669921875

         "bbox": [

          279.8115234375,

     "textlines": [

        {

         "color": 0,
         "alpha": 255,
         "ascender": 0.800000011920929,
         "descender": -0.20000000298023224,
         "text": "A site plan usually is a necessary accompaniment to the single-line diagram for a complete",
         "origin": [
          120.0,
          545.3330078125

         "bbox": [
          120.0,
          538.4775390625,

       ]
      },
      {

        559.5205078125
       ],
       "spans": [
        {
         "size": 10.0,
         "flags": 4,
         "bidi": 0,
         "char_flags": 16,
         "font": "Times-Roman+2",
         "color": 0,
         "alpha": 255,
         "ascender": 0.800000011920929,
         "descender": -0.20000000298023224,
         "text": "description and mapping of the industrial and commercial electric distribution system. The",
         "origin": [
          120.0,
          557.3330078125
         ],
         "bbox": [
          120.0,
          550.4775390625,
          492.0830078125,

         "block": 10

        {

         "font": "Times-Roman+2",
         "color": 0,
         "alpha": 255,
         "ascender": 0.800000011920929,
         "descender": -0.20000000298023224,
         "text": "locations of the major components of the system are usually easy to visualize; however, cir-",
         "origin": [

          569.3330078125

          120.0,
          562.4775390625,
          492.0809631347656,

         "color": 0,
         "alpha": 255,
         "ascender": 0.800000011920929,
         "descender": -0.20000000298023224,
         "text": "cuit routing is difficult to comprehend without a site plan.",
         "origin": [

          350.29901123046875,

         "color": 0,
         "alpha": 255,
         "ascender": 0.800000011920929,
         "descender": -0.20000000298023224,
         "text": "Site plans are important for a number of reasons, all of which could affect the operation of the",
         "origin": [
          120.0,
          608.666015625

          601.810546875,

       "spans": [
        {

         "flags": 4,
         "bidi": 0,

         "font": "Times-Roman+2",
         "color": 0,
         "alpha": 255,
         "ascender": 0.800000011920929,
         "descender": -0.20000000298023224,
         "text": "industrial plant or commercial complex at some time. If a major catastrophe such as fire,",
         "origin": [

         ],
         "bbox": [
          120.0,
          613.166015625,
          492.08905029296875,

         "color": 0,
         "alpha": 255,
         "ascender": 0.800000011920929,
         "descender": -0.20000000298023224,
         "text": "flood, or storm damage should occur, a site plan would be an important tool if the distribution",
         "origin": [

         "color": 0,
         "alpha": 255,
         "ascender": 0.800000011920929,
         "descender": -0.20000000298023224,
         "text": "system were to be reconstructed. The expansion and/or rearrangement of an electrical distri-",
         "origin": [

         "color": 0,

         "descender": -0.20000000298023224,
         "text": "bution system could be extremely difficult without accurate records of the location of existing",
         "origin": [

          656.666015625
         ],
         "bbox": [
          120.0,
          649.166015625,
          492.08502197265625,

         "origin": [
